Load when the user shows a text and judges it, asks whether it reads as AI-written, shows frustration with its wording or offers it as a sample of good writing, said outright or implied. The text may come from an agent, a person or another project. ([bjoern621/claude-skills](https://github.com/bjoern621/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/writing-feedback))
- **writing-fhevm-contracts**: Use when writing, modifying, or reviewing any Solidity contract that uses Zama fhEVM encrypted types (euint, ebool, eaddress) — confidential DeFi, private voting, sealed-bid auctions, encrypted ERC20 tokens, encrypted balances, or any application where on-chain data must remain encrypted under fully homomorphic encryption. Symptoms in code: imports from @fhevm/solidity, uses externalEuintXX or FHE.fromExternal, calls FHE.add/sub/mul/select, FHE.allowThis, FHE.makePubliclyDecryptable, ZamaEthereumConfig, fhevm.createEncryptedInput. Use also when frontend integrates relayer-sdk for client-side… ([0xE1337/fhevm-skill](https://github.com/0xE1337/fhevm-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/fhevm) · ★1)
- **writing-flows**: Writes single-scenario Flow documents under flows/&lt;topic&gt;/ — use case context, one Mermaid diagram (sequence or flowchart), step-by-step branches, and source file references — so how a service behaves end-to-end can be grasped at a glance. Use when user asks for a sequence diagram, user flow, use case doc, or mentions 시퀀스 다이어그램, 유저 플로우, 플로우 그려줘, 동작 흐름, 플로우 작성. Also trigger on intent like 어떻게 동작하는지 한눈에 보고 싶다, 동작 순서 정리해줘 — but not on simple code questions about a single function. ([devstefancho/skills](https://github.com/devstefancho/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/spec-driven/writing-flows) · ★13)
